# Reachable static-state members — resetability annotation

> Machine-generated by the cross-assembly `RestoreStateAnalyzer` (`--annotate-statics`) against the state-5 build
> closure. This is the raw, exhaustive inventory of every static member reachable from the restore entrypoints,
> each assigned a resetability verdict. See `restore-static-state-audit.md` for the curated analysis and the
> hand-adjudication of the attention surface. Regenerate with:
> `dotnet run -c Debug -- <closure.binlog> out.md --annotate-statics=<sha>`.


Total reachable static members: **1357**. Each is assigned exactly one verdict.

| Verdict | Count | Meaning |
| --- | --: | --- |
| ImmutableValue | 711 | primitive/string/enum/immutable ref - a fresh process recreates the identical value |
| StableSingletonOrTable | 421 | stateless helper, comparer, or constant lookup table - recreated identically |
| InputKeyedCache | 173 | memoization keyed purely on inputs - deterministic; only unbounded memory in a reused host |
| ThreadScratch | 7 | [ThreadStatic]/ThreadLocal scratch, cleared on use - no cross-build correctness leak |
| ResetByCleanup | 8 | torn down by RestoreProcessStateCleanup at end of build |
| ResetByTraits | 2 | env-derived; re-read via NuGetTraits at start AND end of restore |
| EnvDerivedNotReset | 8 | caches an environment value but is NOT yet reset - genuine gap |
| LiveResource | 14 | holds a process/timer/socket/semaphore - dispose surface |
| NeedsReview | 13 | mutable global written during restore that no rule could prove safe |

**Attention surface** (EnvDerivedNotReset + LiveResource + NeedsReview): **35** members — enumerated individually below. All others are immutable, deterministic caches, thread scratch, or actively reset.
